# Best of 2024: Master List of Items

Every list in the Best of 2024 directory, grouped by topic, with the items each list names. Ranked lists keep their order (numbered); unranked lists are bulleted. Where a list could not be read, its heading stays with the reason. An overlap section at the end shows items that appear on two or more lists within the same topic.

Lists: 199 total, 167 with items extracted, 32 not retrievable.

## Overlap across lists

Items on two or more lists within the same topic, most lists first. Titles are matched within a category, ignoring case, punctuation, diacritics, a leading The/A/An, edition notes, and season labels. A bare title is merged with a subtitled or lightly misspelled variant when the creators do not conflict. The same title stays split when both creators are known and name different people.
